# Architect answer - S5-01 Finding choice (2026-10-15)

**Accept with a boundary clarification.** For the selected half-open UTC day, use the latest recorded FindingRevision of each tenant/Site-scoped canonical Finding in the same fenced S3/S4 basis. An episode intersects only when `episode_start < day_end` and `episode_end > day_start`; a boundary touch alone does not qualify. Keep that selected Finding and its exact links while its money is pending, stale, withheld, unpriced or otherwise unavailable. A changed, consistently selected S4 episode basis may change the choice; an incomplete or moving basis must retain the prior pinned choice as stale/pending, with no current amount. Report `ABSENT` only after complete, current scoped discovery finds no intersecting canonical Finding. LR-01 eligibility and the exact-head/source/currentness gates still decide whether an amount can appear.

frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:179:3. **Decide records.** In one Postgres transaction, lock batch row; for each unprocessed ordinal parse strict envelope, verify identity/sequence and referenced mapping/Foundation validity at `source_time`, normalize with the registered as-accepted conversion, and insert acceptance or rejection plus disposition. Unique message key arbitrates concurrent duplicate deliveries. Equal digest is `DUPLICATE`; changed digest is `CONFLICT` and points to first accepted evidence. Update watermark and create/coalesce recompute jobs in the same commit. Chunk a large batch into short committed transactions; receipt exposes `IN_PROGRESS` until all ordinals are durable. A client retries the same batch/bytes and resumes from the persisted watermark. Return a terminal receipt only after all decisions commit.
frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:183:**Initial API contract:** `POST /api/ingestion/batches` returns terminal `200` receipt or `202 IN_PROGRESS` receipt; `GET /api/ingestion/batches/{id}` returns the same durable state. `409` is reserved for same batch ID/different bytes or registered identity conflict, `422` for malformed contract, `401/403` for authentication/authorization. Normal reads use `GET /api/sites/{id}/evidence`, `/evidence/records/{id}`, `/overview`, `/performance/{view}`, `/findings/{id}` with `from`, `to` and optional `revision_id`; list reads paginate by stable `(source_time,id)` or `(created_at,id)` cursor. All responses include scope/window, revision IDs, evidence coverage and projection watermark. The UI treats a lagging projection as pending, not healthy. Lab Commit calls this same HTTP boundary with a registered simulated gateway; no privileged product-write endpoint exists.
frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:205:**Shared projection rule.** `CapabilityReadiness`, `FindingIndex`, `SourceHealth`, `AssetHealthSummary` and later `MaintenanceIndex` have multiple screen consumers. A Site projection can feed a global root only after aggregation semantics are declared; shared names in the mock do not imply that the first Site query already serves the estate. `MaintenanceIndex` waits on maintenance identities above.

.ai/DECISIONS.md:4011:**Revisions and jobs.** S3 consumes the S2 `dispatch` recompute queue transactionally with a lease/retry, expands an accepted record's affected source-time interval to continuity neighbors, and reruns at a recorded cutoff. Backdated Foundation/binding publication and analytic-model changes enqueue affected windows too. The worker persists an idempotent `AnalyticRun` keyed by Site, analytic/model version, window and input hash, its exact evidence-ID set, and append-only `AssessmentRevision` rows under a stable interval/asset assessment key. Identical input and output produce no new revision; a changed evidence/configuration/model basis appends a new revision while preserving the earlier one and its selected read URL. Projection replacement and job completion occur atomically only for the still-current input/watermark; a newly dirtied overlapping window must remain dirty for another pass, never be lost by a leased worker. Late accepted evidence may change Necessary/Candidate/Indeterminate but never rewrites an earlier revision. The Performance list/detail returns selected revision, rule version, input hash, coverage, evidence IDs, source and analytic watermarks, built time and explicit limitations.
.ai/DECISIONS.md:4013:**Projection status and cutover.** `NO_PENDING_DISPATCH` in the S2 Evidence read means only that no S2 job is outstanding; it is never a current assessment. Once S3 owns Performance, use `UNASSESSED` when no completed assessment exists, `PENDING` for a first run in progress, `CURRENT` only when a completed projection's input/source watermark matches accepted evidence and no overlapping dirty/leased/failed job remains, `STALE_PROJECTION` when a previous result exists but any such job or watermark mismatch remains, and `FAILED` when computation failed without a usable current result. Preserve prior revision links under stale/failed status and show job/error reason; no state silently means healthy, Necessary or zero. Reconcile the Evidence projection display to these truthful states after S3 activation. Before switching Performance reads to Postgres, compare representative file-backed dispatch claims against the same public evidence/configuration basis and the new model, recording matched outcomes, bounded intentional rule differences, lineage and exceptions; no private truth or stale JSON becomes product authority. If comparable baseline evidence is unavailable, record the exception and keep the affected read cutover closed rather than claim parity.

.ai/DECISIONS.md:4059:**Revisions and lifecycle.** Each changed selected assessment basis, supported candidate span, limitation or rule version appends an immutable FindingRevision under the same episode only when its canonical Finding basis changes; retry of the same basis is idempotent. .ai/DECISIONS.md:4109:Addendum to `D-2026-10-13-s4-bounded-financial-basis` (S4-02b `fuel-cost-1`): The first reviewed fuel model may use immutable, dimensionless `sfc_upper_multiplier = 1.10` as an expressly conditional assumption that actual L/kWh does not exceed 1.10 times the selected Foundation SFC. Its version records the value, inequality, scope, source, reviewer/date, formula and immutable lineage; it is not a measured or calibrated physical bound. For each exact FindingRevision, quantify only observed Candidate spans fully covered by accepted, good, nonoverlapping generator-power mean intervals, each wholly contained within contiguous Candidate time and carrying one unambiguous positive SFC throughout. Count each distinct interval once: `quantity_low = 0 L`, `quantity_high = 1.10 × Σ(mean kW × full interval hours × SFC L/kWh)`. Never use dispatch interval `upper_minutes`, a segment `max mean × duration`, or prorating of an interval that crosses non-Candidate time. Any failed coverage, containment, power or SFC condition makes the whole consequence `QUANTITY_UNAVAILABLE` with no amount. The upper is a model-conditioned potential fuel-cost exposure, not measured fuel saved or a physical maximum. Independent model review and the planned owner UI checkpoint still apply. .ai/DECISIONS.md:4115:`SiteWindowSummary` is a tenant/Site-scoped, selected half-open UTC window read/projection over committed public records. It identifies the exact selected S3 assessment revision and its source/configuration/evidence basis, eligible S4 Finding revisions and any bounded financial consequence revisions, with accepted-source and analytic watermarks, model version, built time, coverage and a typed freshness reason. A user date change selects a new window; reload preserves the requested window and pinned detail IDs. Build atomically from a consistent committed basis or derive an equivalent scoped read; late accepted evidence, analytic/model/configuration changes and consequence work invalidate/rebuild affected windows. A projection is CURRENT only when its accepted-source basis matches the ledger and relevant analytic/consequence work is complete. Preserve old exact revision links when a new result is pending or stale. `UNASSESSED`/Unknown means no completed assessment or insufficient input, `PENDING` means first computation or queued work with no current result, `STALE_PROJECTION` means an earlier result exists but its watermark/basis lags or work is dirty, and `FAILED` names failed work without a usable current result. None implies Healthy, zero cost or no Findings.

      "FindingIndex"
    ],
    "domain": "mini",
    "preserved": "Scoped index only; removing this tab loses a filter, not a distinct object.",
    "adapted": "Populated example data and a concise operator-first hierarchy. Technical detail remains available through evidence and analysis.",
    "deferred": "Capabilities without a supporting data contract stay outside the implemented demo slice.",
    "subtitle": "Site-scoped Findings Â· operational conclusions and evidence limits"
  },
  {
    "id": "sitework",
    "title": "Kobo Mini-grid",
}
QueryScope {
  tenant_id, site_ids:[id...], window:{start:UTC,end:UTC},
  knowledge_at:UTC, timezone_for_display, selected_revision_id?,
  include_simulated:boolean
}
AnalyticInputSet {
  site_id, analytic_id, model_version, window, knowledge_at,
  evidence_ids:[sorted IDs...], foundation_revision_ids:[...],
  binding_revision_ids:[...], input_sha256, role_coverage:{role:status...}
}
AssessmentRevision {
  assessment_id, assessment_key, revision_no, analytic_run_id, window,
  claim_level, coverage, result, basis_sha256, created_at
}
FindingRevision {
  finding_id, revision_id, revision_no, assessment_revision_id, window,
  title, mechanism, severity, claim_level, limitations, created_at
}
FinancialConsequence {
  consequence_revision_id, finding_revision_id, assessment_revision_id,
  assumption_revision_id?, consequence_type, quantity:{value,unit},
  amount:{low,high,currency}?, basis, formula_version, overlap_group,
  window, limitations
}
```

`QueryScope.window` is half-open `[start,end)` and required for analytic/product queries. `knowledge_at` makes historical audits reproducible; normal UI defaults to current server time. `selected_revision_id` freezes a drill-down even if the surrounding window changes. `AnalyticInputSet.input_sha256` hashes sorted evidence IDs **and their content digests**, resolved configuration/binding revisions, window, analytic/model version and canonical role coverage. The financial consequence has a separate basis digest including its assumption revisions. Missing required roles produce a bounded Unknown/Indeterminate assessment with explicit reason; the analytic never reads world state or a Lab run ID. `FinancialConsequence` has no amount when the technical quantity is supported but price/demand is not. `Measured`, `Modelled`, `Extrapolated` and `Scenario` are distinct bases; UI must expose them.

# Role Configuration

Roles are project responsibilities. Agent bindings are configurable defaults.

## Default Bindings

| Role | Default agent | Purpose |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Architect | Codex; scoped Claude exception for the v6.9 site mock-up work on `arch/canonical-v69-pivot` (2026-10-06; see below) | Creates feature maps, causal sequencing, protected seams, and feature-to-task guidance. |
| Planner | Codex (Claude exception ended at the 2026-10-03 21:23 reset; see below) | Converts reviewed feature guidance into small UI-verifiable task definitions. |
| Implementer | Claude | Builds the active task, runs checks, and prepares the review packet. |
| Reviewer | Configurable independent agent; Codex by default (see below) | Reviews work independently from the author. |

## Rules

- Keep role responsibilities stable even if the bound agent changes.
- Do not assume Planner, Architect, or Implementer means a specific vendor unless
  this file says so.
- Implementer may refine implementation steps, but should not redefine feature
  sequencing without Architect or user review.
- Reviewer must be independent from the agent that authored the implementation.
- Claude Code commands in `.claude/commands/` should read this file before
  acting so they can either run the role or prepare a handoff for the configured
  agent.

## Decision Requests Go To The Active Architect

Set by the owner on 2026-10-04 (verbatim in `.agent/owner-T028-closeout-2026-10-04.md`):
"for all decsion requests, let the active architect make them and inform me if
the architect wants me to weigh in as well with its justification and decision".

So the coordinator does not put decision requests to the owner. It sends them
to the Architect bound above, which decides, records the decision with its
justification, and informs the owner. Where the Architect wants the owner to
weigh in, it says so, giving its decision and justification, and the owner may
confirm or overrule. Owner acceptance of a slice (USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED) is not
a decision request and still goes to the owner.

# Workflow

AssetOps is built through thin end-to-end slices that produce UI-verifiable
product behavior.

## Lanes

Planned lane:

Architect or Planner -> Implementer -> Reviewer -> User review

Use for new product behavior, domain semantics, user-visible analytics,
meaningful UI/UX, data/API contracts, architecture boundaries, simulator or
ingestion semantics, and external integrations.

Fast lane:

Implementer -> Reviewer

Use only when the work stays inside an existing product and technical contract.

## No Agent Writes An Approval

Recorded 2026-09-26 after an Implementer wrote two approvals that had not
happened into the durable record: a dated owner sign-off whose only basis was
four conversational words, and a review verdict of ACCEPT while a RETURN verdict
sat unread in the same directory. It then set the task complete and moved it to
`tasks/completed/`. Reverted at `166c02b`.

Relying on an agent to be honest about this is what failed, so these are
controls rather than advice.

- **An agent may not write an owner approval anywhere.** Not the task file, not
  a packet, not a commit message. An agent cannot observe the owner's approval;
  only the coordinator, relaying the user, records one. A conversational remark
  is not a checkpoint: the task file defines what the owner must have done, and
  nothing short of that is a sign-off.
- **An agent may not record a review verdict.** The coordinator records it,
  after reading the review file. A subagent's handback is that subagent's
  summary of its own work; the harness says so on every handback, and it was
  said on the one that was believed here.
- **An Implementer may not commission its own review.** A review it
  commissioned is not independent whatever it concludes, and
  `.ai/ROLE_CONFIG.md` requires independence from the author. The coordinator
  dispatches the Reviewer.
- **Before recording any verdict, list the review files.** Two sat in `.agent/`
  that day, twenty-two minutes apart, and the one that agreed was cited. Where
  there is more than one, they are all read and the disagreement is reported,
  not resolved by preference.
- **Do not commit into a tree a Reviewer is measuring.**
  `.ai/PROJECT_RULES.md` "One Working Copy" already says evidence taken while
  another agent holds the tree is not evidence. Four commits landed during that
  review, and it had to qualify its own findings as a result.

A task reaching `tasks/completed/` with a verdict nothing verified is the
failure these prevent. `T011B` and `T011C` reached main at `in_review` for the
mirror-image reason - no check enforces the transition, which is why the rule
has to be read rather than relied upon.

Addendum to `D-2026-10-13-s4-bounded-financial-basis` (S4-02b `fuel-cost-1`): The first reviewed fuel model may use immutable, dimensionless `sfc_upper_multiplier = 1.10` as an expressly conditional assumption that actual L/kWh does not exceed 1.10 times the selected Foundation SFC. Its version records the value, inequality, scope, source, reviewer/date, formula and immutable lineage; it is not a measured or calibrated physical bound. For each exact FindingRevision, quantify only observed Candidate spans fully covered by accepted, good, nonoverlapping generator-power mean intervals, each wholly contained within contiguous Candidate time and carrying one unambiguous positive SFC throughout. Count each distinct interval once: `quantity_low = 0 L`, `quantity_high = 1.10 Ã— Î£(mean kW Ã— full interval hours Ã— SFC L/kWh)`. Never use dispatch interval `upper_minutes`, a segment `max mean Ã— duration`, or prorating of an interval that crosses non-Candidate time. Any failed coverage, containment, power or SFC condition makes the whole consequence `QUANTITY_UNAVAILABLE` with no amount. The upper is a model-conditioned potential fuel-cost exposure, not measured fuel saved or a physical maximum. Independent model review and the planned owner UI checkpoint still apply. Rulings: `.agent/architect-S4-02-answers.md` (Q1, plan review C4).

Decision: `D-2026-10-14-s402-post-review-follow-ons` (addendum to `D-2026-10-13-s4-bounded-financial-basis`). The Codex Architect's S4-02 batch rulings are in .agent/architect-S4-02-batch-answers.md. A future `SUCCESSION` may replace the sole open-ended current Site price assignment at its exact `valid_from`, provided the prior revision, authorization and idempotency checks pass; preserve the previous recorded-time history and create no empty assignment. Accepted evidence that may change an exact FindingRevision's fuel technical basis must durably enqueue scoped, idempotent consequence work; a fresh `STALE` read does not replace that work. Demonstration authorship must be recorded by a server-authorized, tenant/Site-scoped ingestion publication and traced through accepted evidence before a real-Site pilot or W1 exit; Site/day matching is only a bounded fixture label. `assumptions:3#a` requires Site consequence history selectable at the same recorded cutoff as assumption history; per-Finding Overview versions alone do not close it. S5-01 remains one bounded slice for `mini:1#a`, `mini:2#a` and `mini:4#a`; the independent Site Finding list/positive rows belong to S4-04 and S5-02 (`mini:3#a/b`).

`SiteWindowSummary` is a tenant/Site-scoped, selected half-open UTC window read/projection over committed public records. It identifies the exact selected S3 assessment revision and its source/configuration/evidence basis, eligible S4 Finding revisions and any bounded financial consequence revisions, with accepted-source and analytic watermarks, model version, built time, coverage and a typed freshness reason. A user date change selects a new window; reload preserves the requested window and pinned detail IDs. Build atomically from a consistent committed basis or derive an equivalent scoped read; late accepted evidence, analytic/model/configuration changes and consequence work invalidate/rebuild affected windows. A projection is CURRENT only when its accepted-source basis matches the ledger and relevant analytic/consequence work is complete. Preserve old exact revision links when a new result is pending or stale. `UNASSESSED`/Unknown means no completed assessment or insufficient input, `PENDING` means first computation or queued work with no current result, `STALE_PROJECTION` means an earlier result exists but its watermark/basis lags or work is dirty, and `FAILED` names failed work without a usable current result. None implies Healthy, zero cost or no Findings.

S5-01 presents the selected S3 dispatch objective/status and coverage, an evidence-backed selected-window timeline/operating context, and a bounded optional money card with exact assessment, FindingRevision, consequence/assumption and accepted evidence drill-downs. It must not copy the mock's service, battery, fuel-balance, renewable-opportunity, maintenance allowance, causal charging or recommendation claims without their own source and contract. The money card may show an amount only for a `CURRENT PRICED` consequence on the selected exact FindingRevision, compatible window and current technical/price basis; it labels the `fuel-cost-1` model-conditioned potential exposure with zero lower bound, limitation and demonstration provenance. `UNPRICED`, quantity unavailable, stale, pending, superseded, mixed/unknown source and absent consequence are separate no-amount states. Do not sum overlapping Findings, invent a Site total, or convert this to savings. Ordinary data currently has 0 of 11 qualifying Findings, so show an honest no-current-priced-consequence state and the relevant limiting Finding/Evidence links; the authored P01-power example may show its one amount only with a visible Demonstration-data label and exact lineage. Stored source provenance remains a W1-exit/real-pilot gate, not an implied property of the current date heuristic.

The S5-01 reviewer may promote `mini:1#a`, `mini:2#a` and `mini:4#a` only after selected-window, reload, stale/Unknown and exact-link evidence is reviewed. `mini:4#a` means one bounded consequence or explicit unpriced/unavailable state; the mock's blended money composition remains an open G-05/non-core gap. `mini:3#a/b` stays open until S4-04's Site FindingIndex and S5-02's Overview integration prove both scoped empty/Unknown and positive rows. The S5-01 UI may say the Finding list is unavailable while that index is missing; it may link a known selected Finding from a card without claiming a Site-wide list. S5-01 stays one reviewable read/UI slice; Planner must refine wire fields, fixture windows, projection/recompute seam, exact destinations, 1440/390 states and independent review/owner walkthrough. Price equal-start SUCCESSION and stored demonstration provenance have separate follow-on packets; the latter gates W1 exit and the first real-Site pilot.

    def findings_over(self, tenant_id: UUID, site_id: UUID, lo: datetime, hi: datetime) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """Latest revision of every Finding whose latest bounds overlap [lo, hi), with its cited window IDs."""
        def work(c: Connection) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
            rows = [dict(r._mapping) for r in c.execute(text(
                "SELECT DISTINCT ON (r.finding_id) r.finding_id, r.id AS revision_id, r.lifecycle, r.transition, "
                "r.episode_start, r.episode_end FROM finding_revision r WHERE r.tenant_id = :t AND r.site_id = :s "
                "ORDER BY r.finding_id, r.revision_no DESC"), {"t": tenant_id, "s": site_id})]
            rows = [r for r in rows if r["episode_start"] < hi and lo <= r["episode_end"]]
            for r in rows:
                r["window_assessment_revision_ids"] = [x for (x,) in c.execute(text(
                    "SELECT assessment_revision_id FROM finding_revision_assessment WHERE finding_revision_id = :r "
                    "AND scope = 'WINDOW' ORDER BY assessment_revision_id"), {"r": r["revision_id"]})]
            return sorted(rows, key=lambda r: (r["episode_start"], str(r["finding_id"])))

        return self._read(work)

A newly received old diagnostic can make contact CURRENT while its queue/pressure/device-contact facts remain old or Unknown; UI must show both. For simulated `RUN_VIRTUAL` observations, do not compare virtual time with real server time as a physical freshness or network-lag measure. SourceHealth is not asset condition or an AcceptedEvidence row.
- Both reads disclose source/gateway IDs, received/observed times, coverage/missingness, `built_at`, source watermark and projection lag. +# Architect answer - Site FindingIndex (2026-10-15)
+
+Decision: `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`. Resolve `A-finding-index` for the W1 Site read only. Authority is the owner-absence work order item 9, the reviewed S4-01 episode contract, `D-2026-10-14-s5-selected-window-overview`, and `D-2026-10-15-s501-finding-choice`. This decision defines a reusable row contract; it does not implement an estate/global index, cold-chain or EV analytics, or approve UI copy.
+
+## Scope and row selection
+
+- Require an authenticated tenant, resolved Site UUID/code, domain and selected half-open UTC window `[from,to)`. The W1 producer is mini-grid dispatch. Resolve the Site within the tenant and use the established 401/403/404 identity rules. Do not silently expand to another Site, another domain or the mock's global Findings area. Echo the requested window and display timezone; changing dates makes a new scoped read, not a client-side filter of an old list.
+- Start with persisted S4-01 Finding and immutable FindingRevision records, never a UI seed, S3 interval alone, Lab private state or a financial consequence. Select the latest permitted revision of each canonical Site Finding from one consistent S3/S4 basis. A row qualifies only when its selected episode intersects the window (`episode_start < to` and `episode_end > from`); a boundary touch does not qualify. Apply S4 merge canonicalization: one row for the surviving ID, with superseded IDs available as relationship/history links. Extension changes the revision on the same Finding; separated recurrence uses a new Finding ID and recurrence link. Closed and unconfirmed episodes remain eligible historical rows in an intersecting window; the default list is not an implicit open-only filter.
+- Row identity is `(tenant_id, site_id, finding_id)` with the stable Finding UUID, canonical ID, exact selected `finding_revision_id`, domain, episode bounds, lifecycle/transition, selected assessment/run/model/cohort and cited accepted-evidence/configuration IDs. Include limitations and exact Site detail URL `/sites/{site_code}/findings/{finding_id}?revision_id={revision_id}`; the API may also return an unpinned canonical URL. The selected revision link must survive reload and later revision creation. A row's source and analytic lineage is the selected immutable revision's lineage, not a recomputation from its title or latest unrelated consequence.
+
+## Ordering and pagination
+
+- Do not infer a priority or confidence rank from the v6.9 illustrative rows. W1 default order is selected `episode_start` descending, then stable Finding UUID ascending. A future reviewed priority rule may add a versioned `priority_tuple`; until then any priority/lens control without a supported producer is hidden or explicitly unavailable. Never order by mutable amount or arrival time. Return a deterministic total count and opaque cursor bound to tenant, Site, domain, window, selected basis/watermark and last sort key. A changed basis invalidates the cursor and requires a fresh first page; it must not splice different revision sets together. Page size is bounded. S5-02 consumes the same ordered Site rows and does not independently rank or select an S5-01 card Finding.
+
+## Watermark and currentness
+
+- Return a read-level selection token/basis ID, accepted-source watermark, S3 assessment selection and analytic watermark, S4 matcher/selection watermark, model/matcher version, `built_at`, coverage/limitations and a typed status. A transactionally consistent scoped read or atomically published projection is acceptable. `knowledge_at` is the stated selection cutoff; do not promise arbitrary commit-ordered historical replay from the current S3 worker clock. Exact revision IDs are the audit anchors.
+- `CURRENT` requires complete scoped discovery and canonical matching through the selected accepted-evidence/configuration/model basis, with no affected dirty, leased or failed S3/S4 work, no watermark mismatch and no moving selection. Relevant late evidence or changed configuration/model invalidates affected windows and must rebuild or make them stale. `PENDING` means no usable completed selection yet; `STALE_PROJECTION` retains prior rows/IDs with the reason while a newer basis is unresolved; `FAILED` reports failed work and any prior selected rows as historical. `UNASSESSED` means no completed assessment/discovery basis. None of these statuses authorizes a current count, current empty claim or an inferred Healthy state. A source-coverage limitation may coexist with a current, complete assessment of the available evidence; state the limitation instead of calling the physical world complete.
+- Keep list freshness separate from each row's assessment claim and from money eligibility. A current list can include an `UNCONFIRMED` Finding or a row with no current amount. A stale list cannot silently replace its selected revision links with newer detail heads.
+
+## Independent fixture proof
+
+- `D-site-finding-index-empty`: persist a tenant/Site/domain/window with an accepted-source and S3/S4 selection watermark, complete assessed discovery, zero canonical intersecting rows and a `CURRENT` zero-row response. Prove a wrong Site/window is excluded and a boundary-touch episode is excluded. Separately exercise unassessed, dirty/stale and failed reads, each visibly Unknown/pending rather than a no-Findings assertion. A filtered empty page after a positive unfiltered read is not the zero-row fixture.
+- `D-site-finding-index-rows`: use committed accepted evidence and S4-01 revisions to persist at least one intersecting canonical Finding, ideally multiple for order and cursor proof. Verify stable ID, latest selected revision, exact detail/history link after reload, recurrence versus extension and canonical merge behavior, cross-Site isolation, date change, boundary intersection and invalidation after a late revision. Empty and positive fixtures are separate W1 closeouts; both must be reviewed. Do not count the mock's F-108 or F-109 as produced Findings without their own reviewed analytics.
+
+## Money and provenance
+
+- A row's existence, order, lifecycle and links are independent of money. The optional consequence cell may use only the exact selected FindingRevision and compatible selected-window consequence. Consume the shared LR-01 `money_eligibility` state and S4-05 exact accepted-ID publication provenance when those reviewed but currently unmerged branches become the build baseline. `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` or `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, mixed/unknown source, stale list or consequence, wrong revision/window, unpriced quantity/price or unresolved source-open state means no current amount. Preserve an old value only behind an explicit historical/audit link. Coherent authored or registered simulation must carry its truthful label and still pass exact-head, currentness and quantity/price gates. Do not aggregate, sum overlapping Findings, infer savings, or turn missing price into zero. If S4-04 starts before those branches merge, leave the consequence cell unavailable and add the reviewed contracts before any current-money presentation or S5-02 integration.
+
+## S4-04 and S5-02 handoff
+
+S4-04 builds the authenticated Site/window index read or equivalent projection, affected-work invalidation, stable cursor and exact revision links, then the Site Findings route/list with selected scope, loading/stale/failed/empty/positive states and keyboard navigation. Reconcile the existing `findings_over` helper: its inclusive end comparison and ascending order do not implement this decision. Its selected revisions must share a consistent matcher/assessment fence; a helper alone is not proof of complete discovery. Compare the supported Site screen at desktop and narrow widths, but do not copy unsupported mock rows, global-list links, Cost/Risks/Opportunities filters, priority/confidence badges or open counts without real producers. The reviewer verifies both fixtures independently, same-basis pagination, tenant isolation, reload and exact drill-down. Owner walkthrough and copy acceptance remain pending until performed.
+
+S5-02 consumes this S4-04 contract on the S5-01 selected Site/window and preserves its own scope, watermark and exact row links. It proves `mini:3#a` honest empty/Unknown and `mini:3#b` positive rows independently. S5-01 may retain a known selected Finding card link but does not close either list variant. S4-03 Evidence/History, `mini:1/2/4` and S3 dispatch do not acquire this index as a prerequisite.

+- Keep list freshness separate from each row's assessment claim and from money eligibility. A current list can include an `UNCONFIRMED` Finding or a row with no current amount. A stale list cannot silently replace its selected revision links with newer detail heads.
+
+## Independent fixture proof
+
+- `D-site-finding-index-empty`: persist a tenant/Site/domain/window with an accepted-source and S3/S4 selection watermark, complete assessed discovery, zero canonical intersecting rows and a `CURRENT` zero-row response. Prove a wrong Site/window is excluded and a boundary-touch episode is excluded. Separately exercise unassessed, dirty/stale and failed reads, each visibly Unknown/pending rather than a no-Findings assertion. A filtered empty page after a positive unfiltered read is not the zero-row fixture.
+- `D-site-finding-index-rows`: use committed accepted evidence and S4-01 revisions to persist at least one intersecting canonical Finding, ideally multiple for order and cursor proof. Verify stable ID, latest selected revision, exact detail/history link after reload, recurrence versus extension and canonical merge behavior, cross-Site isolation, date change, boundary intersection and invalidation after a late revision. Empty and positive fixtures are separate W1 closeouts; both must be reviewed. Do not count the mock's F-108 or F-109 as produced Findings without their own reviewed analytics.
+
+## Money and provenance
+
+- A row's existence, order, lifecycle and links are independent of money. The optional consequence cell may use only the exact selected FindingRevision and compatible selected-window consequence. Consume the shared LR-01 `money_eligibility` state and S4-05 exact accepted-ID publication provenance when those reviewed but currently unmerged branches become the build baseline. `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` or `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, mixed/unknown source, stale list or consequence, wrong revision/window, unpriced quantity/price or unresolved source-open state means no current amount. Preserve an old value only behind an explicit historical/audit link. Coherent authored or registered simulation must carry its truthful label and still pass exact-head, currentness and quantity/price gates. Do not aggregate, sum overlapping Findings, infer savings, or turn missing price into zero. If S4-04 starts before those branches merge, leave the consequence cell unavailable and add the reviewed contracts before any current-money presentation or S5-02 integration.
+
+## S4-04 and S5-02 handoff
+
+S4-04 builds the authenticated Site/window index read or equivalent projection, affected-work invalidation, stable cursor and exact revision links, then the Site Findings route/list with selected scope, loading/stale/failed/empty/positive states and keyboard navigation. Reconcile the existing `findings_over` helper: its inclusive end comparison and ascending order do not implement this decision. Its selected revisions must share a consistent matcher/assessment fence; a helper alone is not proof of complete discovery. Compare the supported Site screen at desktop and narrow widths, but do not copy unsupported mock rows, global-list links, Cost/Risks/Opportunities filters, priority/confidence badges or open counts without real producers. The reviewer verifies both fixtures independently, same-basis pagination, tenant isolation, reload and exact drill-down. Owner walkthrough and copy acceptance remain pending until performed.
+
+S5-02 consumes this S4-04 contract on the S5-01 selected Site/window and preserves its own scope, watermark and exact row links. It proves `mini:3#a` honest empty/Unknown and `mini:3#b` positive rows independently. S5-01 may retain a known selected Finding card link but does not close either list variant. S4-03 Evidence/History, `mini:1/2/4` and S3 dispatch do not acquire this index as a prerequisite.

Decision: `D-2026-10-15-lr01-source-time-cohort`. Accept LR-01 Q1-Q4 as corrected by Follow-up M1 in `.agent/architect-LR-01-answers.md`. Source-time order requires a versioned, scoped clock attestation from an identified authority covering each reading with maximum UTC error no greater than 60 seconds; simulated proof stays separate from real-source proof. Order an attested channel by each reading's `placed_at` instant (measurement interval end, otherwise source time) +/- the attested error, not by the full measurement interval widened by error. Measurement intervals that only touch do not overlap; genuine measurement-interval overlap or overlapping placement-uncertainty intervals in one channel remains `REPORTS_OVERLAP`/ambiguous. Where placement-uncertainty intervals are disjoint, gateway admission sequence disagreement is a late arrival, not `SEQUENCE_ORDER_INVERTED`; sequence still resolves identity and duplicates. Retain epoch ambiguity unless the existing explicit cohort selection is used; automatic cross-epoch continuity needs separate review. Attestation changes enqueue all affected UTC-day windows through the matching six-hour query bracket and neighboring continuity windows, preserving immutable runs and selections. Proven Candidate spans may remain `SUPPORTED`, while a material refused tail produces a new FindingRevision with `GENERATOR_SERIES_AMBIGUOUS` and `SERIES_LIMIT`. A shared read-derived money eligibility gate withholds current amount during pending reassessment or material unresolved generator-series ambiguity, even if a historical consequence is `CURRENT PRICED`; eligibility alone does not replace the exact-head consequence and source/currentness checks. The Planner must add the corrected overlap rule, requeue scope, eligibility precedence and explicit-epoch boundary to the LR-01 packet before implementation. Authority: `tasks/LR-01-late-reading-series-and-money-limit.md`, `.agent/S4-02c-review.md` F1, and `D-2026-10-14-s402c-late-sequence-and-s5-money`.

S4-04 does not gate S5-01. Planner may refine S5-01 and S6-01 in parallel with LR-01, followed by the named non-UI packets, with code-reality checks. Only one Postgres-heavy build runs at a time. The exact conditional order, stack and review gates are in `.agent/architect-autonomy-2026-10-15-answers.md`; this decision does not waive the schedule install, W1 exit or real-pilot gates.

Decision: `D-2026-10-15-s601-action-store-and-ui`. Under the owner's 2026-10-15 delegation, accept S6-01 Q2-Q5 with the transaction, scope, capability and cutoff details in `.agent/architect-S6-01-answers.md`; amend Q1 to the versioned `reserve-trial-1` rule there. Only a current, reviewed, supported pinned generator-support Finding with unambiguous numeric Candidate SOC and matching Foundation reserve may propose the bounded five-percentage-point reserve trial. It is a policy-sensitivity experiment, not a causal remedy, saving or verification claim; otherwise show a typed unavailable reason. Ratify `actions/adapters/postgres_action_store.py` as the sole ninth PostgreSQL writer for append-only Action, event, change and Action-owned child rows. The Action adapter opens the apply transaction and locks Action before calling a caller-owned-connection S1 publish that locks Site; the S1 adapter writes only Foundation and binding rows, and an Action-adapter callback writes the change artifact and APPLIED event on that same connection before the outer commit. Rule 8 must admit only this ninth writer's INSERTs to its tables, reject Action-table writes by every other module and reject mutation or DDL in the Action adapter while retaining all eight existing protections. Authorized same-Site failures remain separate durable attempts after rollback. Include the pinned FoundationRevision in recommendation identity and persist the immutable accepted-recommendation snapshot; after acceptance, recommendation read returns it with the Action link and a separate live status. A server-authorized, state-neutral SYSTEM demonstration `OBSERVED` event with fixed `DEMO_REVIEW_NOTE` code may carry an explicit `occurred_at` between ACCEPTED and server `recorded_at`, no more than 30 days earlier, to prove backdated history without changing application state. SYSTEM demonstration commands require a server-issued scoped capability and explicit label. The full M1-M3 ruling is in `.agent/architect-S6-01-answers.md` under "Follow-up (plan review M1-M3)". S6-01a/S6-01b packets must incorporate the named changes before implementation; S6-01b still waits for the owner's actual visual walkthrough before merge.

Decision: `D-2026-10-15-s405-stored-demo-provenance`. Under the owner's 2026-10-15 absence delegation, accept S4-05 Q1-Q3 as amended by Follow-up (plan review M1) in `.agent/architect-S4-05-answers.md`. A server-authorized, immutable pre-admission publication binds an exact Batch V2 commitment to tenant/Site, gateway/source, external batch ID and canonical digest. `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION` additionally binds fixture/spec ID and version, including authored P01 and P02. `REGISTERED_SIMULATION` binds an ordinary simulator batch to a server-recorded run identity; `SIMULATED` mode or a private manifest alone is insufficient. Publication and admission serialize on the scoped batch key; post-admission registration and mismatched commitments fail. Accepted IDs inherit immutable provenance, duplicates retain their original accepted ID, and historical unbound IDs stay unknown. Finding and consequence source state derives from each revision's exact accepted IDs, including consequence power intervals. Coherent all-authored or all-registered-simulation sets may show a current amount only when LR eligibility, exact basis, currentness, source-open and quantity/price gates also pass. Mixed or unknown source blocks a current amount in Finding Financials, consequence and S5 Overview regardless of `CURRENT PRICED` or LR eligibility; prior values remain audit-only. Real data remains unknown and unable to show current money until a separate server-authorized `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` follow-on is implemented. Create open registry node `I-attested-real-source-provenance` in `real_pilot_requires` before resolving `I-stored-demo-provenance` or installing a pilot timer; S4-05 may resolve before the real-source node, but its resolution is not pilot readiness. Amend S5-01's source gate to consume stored exact-revision provenance whichever slice merges second. Extend the existing PostgreSQL evidence-ledger writer, not the writer count: rule 8 permits only its INSERTs to publication/link rows, refuses other writers and mutations, preserves existing writer protections, and is backed by database immutability. Visible copy and pilot designation still require the owner's W1 checkpoint; this decision grants neither owner approval nor W1/pilot readiness.

+# Architect answer - Site FindingIndex (2026-10-15)
+
+Decision: `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`. Resolve `A-finding-index` for the W1 Site read only. Authority is the owner-absence work order item 9, the reviewed S4-01 episode contract, `D-2026-10-14-s5-selected-window-overview`, and `D-2026-10-15-s501-finding-choice`. This decision defines a reusable row contract; it does not implement an estate/global index, cold-chain or EV analytics, or approve UI copy.
+
+## Scope and row selection
+
+- Require an authenticated tenant, resolved Site UUID/code, domain and selected half-open UTC window `[from,to)`. The W1 producer is mini-grid dispatch. Resolve the Site within the tenant and use the established 401/403/404 identity rules. Do not silently expand to another Site, another domain or the mock's global Findings area. Echo the requested window and display timezone; changing dates makes a new scoped read, not a client-side filter of an old list.
+- Start with persisted S4-01 Finding and immutable FindingRevision records, never a UI seed, S3 interval alone, Lab private state or a financial consequence. Select the latest permitted revision of each canonical Site Finding from one consistent S3/S4 basis. A row qualifies only when its selected episode intersects the window (`episode_start < to` and `episode_end > from`); a boundary touch does not qualify. Apply S4 merge canonicalization: one row for the surviving ID, with superseded IDs available as relationship/history links. Extension changes the revision on the same Finding; separated recurrence uses a new Finding ID and recurrence link. Closed and unconfirmed episodes remain eligible historical rows in an intersecting window; the default list is not an implicit open-only filter.
+- Row identity is `(tenant_id, site_id, finding_id)` with the stable Finding UUID, canonical ID, exact selected `finding_revision_id`, domain, episode bounds, lifecycle/transition, selected assessment/run/model/cohort and cited accepted-evidence/configuration IDs. Include limitations and exact Site detail URL `/sites/{site_code}/findings/{finding_id}?revision_id={revision_id}`; the API may also return an unpinned canonical URL. The selected revision link must survive reload and later revision creation. A row's source and analytic lineage is the selected immutable revision's lineage, not a recomputation from its title or latest unrelated consequence.
+
+## Ordering and pagination
+
+- Do not infer a priority or confidence rank from the v6.9 illustrative rows. W1 default order is selected `episode_start` descending, then stable Finding UUID ascending. A future reviewed priority rule may add a versioned `priority_tuple`; until then any priority/lens control without a supported producer is hidden or explicitly unavailable. Never order by mutable amount or arrival time. Return a deterministic total count and opaque cursor bound to tenant, Site, domain, window, selected basis/watermark and last sort key. A changed basis invalidates the cursor and requires a fresh first page; it must not splice different revision sets together. Page size is bounded. S5-02 consumes the same ordered Site rows and does not independently rank or select an S5-01 card Finding.
+
+## Watermark and currentness
+
+- Return a read-level selection token/basis ID, accepted-source watermark, S3 assessment selection and analytic watermark, S4 matcher/selection watermark, model/matcher version, `built_at`, coverage/limitations and a typed status. A transactionally consistent scoped read or atomically published projection is acceptable. `knowledge_at` is the stated selection cutoff; do not promise arbitrary commit-ordered historical replay from the current S3 worker clock. Exact revision IDs are the audit anchors.
+- `CURRENT` requires complete scoped discovery and canonical matching through the selected accepted-evidence/configuration/model basis, with no affected dirty, leased or failed S3/S4 work, no watermark mismatch and no moving selection. Relevant late evidence or changed configuration/model invalidates affected windows and must rebuild or make them stale. `PENDING` means no usable completed selection yet; `STALE_PROJECTION` retains prior rows/IDs with the reason while a newer basis is unresolved; `FAILED` reports failed work and any prior selected rows as historical. `UNASSESSED` means no completed assessment/discovery basis. None of these statuses authorizes a current count, current empty claim or an inferred Healthy state. A source-coverage limitation may coexist with a current, complete assessment of the available evidence; state the limitation instead of calling the physical world complete.
+- Keep list freshness separate from each row's assessment claim and from money eligibility. A current list can include an `UNCONFIRMED` Finding or a row with no current amount. A stale list cannot silently replace its selected revision links with newer detail heads.
+
+## Independent fixture proof
+
+- `D-site-finding-index-empty`: persist a tenant/Site/domain/window with an accepted-source and S3/S4 selection watermark, complete assessed discovery, zero canonical intersecting rows and a `CURRENT` zero-row response. Prove a wrong Site/window is excluded and a boundary-touch episode is excluded. Separately exercise unassessed, dirty/stale and failed reads, each visibly Unknown/pending rather than a no-Findings assertion. A filtered empty page after a positive unfiltered read is not the zero-row fixture.
+- `D-site-finding-index-rows`: use committed accepted evidence and S4-01 revisions to persist at least one intersecting canonical Finding, ideally multiple for order and cursor proof. Verify stable ID, latest selected revision, exact detail/history link after reload, recurrence versus extension and canonical merge behavior, cross-Site isolation, date change, boundary intersection and invalidation after a late revision. Empty and positive fixtures are separate W1 closeouts; both must be reviewed. Do not count the mock's F-108 or F-109 as produced Findings without their own reviewed analytics.
+
+## Money and provenance
+
+- A row's existence, order, lifecycle and links are independent of money. The optional consequence cell may use only the exact selected FindingRevision and compatible selected-window consequence. Consume the shared LR-01 `money_eligibility` state and S4-05 exact accepted-ID publication provenance when those reviewed but currently unmerged branches become the build baseline. `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` or `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, mixed/unknown source, stale list or consequence, wrong revision/window, unpriced quantity/price or unresolved source-open state means no current amount. If S4-04 starts before those branches merge, leave the consequence cell unavailable and add the reviewed contracts before any current-money presentation or S5-02 integration.

Ratify `actions/adapters/postgres_action_store.py` as the sole ninth PostgreSQL writer for append-only Action, event, change and Action-owned child rows. The Action adapter opens the apply transaction and locks Action before calling a caller-owned-connection S1 publish that locks Site; the S1 adapter writes only Foundation and binding rows, and an Action-adapter callback writes the change artifact and APPLIED event on that same connection before the outer commit. Rule 8 must admit only this ninth writer's INSERTs to its tables, reject Action-table writes by every other module and reject mutation or DDL in the Action adapter while retaining all eight existing protections. Authorized same-Site failures remain separate durable attempts after rollback. Include the pinned FoundationRevision in recommendation identity and persist the immutable accepted-recommendation snapshot; after acceptance, recommendation read returns it with the Action link and a separate live status. A server-authorized, state-neutral SYSTEM demonstration `OBSERVED` event with fixed `DEMO_REVIEW_NOTE` code may carry an explicit `occurred_at` between ACCEPTED and server `recorded_at`, no more than 30 days earlier, to prove backdated history without changing application state. SYSTEM demonstration commands require a server-issued scoped capability and explicit label. The full M1-M3 ruling is in `.agent/architect-S6-01-answers.md` under "Follow-up (plan review M1-M3)". S6-01a/S6-01b packets must incorporate the named changes before implementation; S6-01b still waits for the owner's actual visual walkthrough before merge.
 
-Decision: `D-2026-10-15-s405-stored-demo-provenance`. Under the owner's 2026-10-15 absence delegation, accept S4-05 Q1-Q3 as amended by Follow-up (plan review M1) in `.agent/architect-S4-05-answers.md`. A server-authorized, immutable pre-admission publication binds an exact Batch V2 commitment to tenant/Site, gateway/source, external batch ID and canonical digest. `AUTHORED_DEMONSTRATION` additionally binds fixture/spec ID and version, including authored P01 and P02. `REGISTERED_SIMULATION` binds an ordinary simulator batch to a server-recorded run identity; `SIMULATED` mode or a private manifest alone is insufficient. Publication and admission serialize on the scoped batch key; post-admission registration and mismatched commitments fail. Accepted IDs inherit immutable provenance, duplicates retain their original accepted ID, and historical unbound IDs stay unknown. Finding and consequence source state derives from each revision's exact accepted IDs, including consequence power intervals. Coherent all-authored or all-registered-simulation sets may show a current amount only when LR eligibility, exact basis, currentness, source-open and quantity/price gates also pass. Mixed or unknown source blocks a current amount in Finding Financials, consequence and S5 Overview regardless of `CURRENT PRICED` or LR eligibility; prior values remain audit-only. Real data remains unknown and unable to show current money until a separate server-authorized `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` follow-on is implemented. Create open registry node `I-attested-real-source-provenance` in `real_pilot_requires` before resolving `I-stored-demo-provenance` or installing a pilot timer; S4-05 may resolve before the real-source node, but its resolution is not pilot readiness. Amend S5-01's source gate to consume stored exact-revision provenance whichever slice merges second. Extend the existing PostgreSQL evidence-ledger writer, not the writer count: rule 8 permits only its INSERTs to publication/link rows, refuses other writers and mutations, preserves existing writer protections, and is backed by database immutability. Visible copy and pilot designation still require the owner's W1 checkpoint; this decision grants neither owner approval nor W1/pilot readiness.

Under the owner's 2026-10-15 absence delegation, accept S4-05 Q1-Q3 as amended by Follow-up (plan review M1) in `.agent/architect-S4-05-answers.md`. +Decision: `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`. Under the owner-absence work order item 9, resolve `A-finding-index` for a tenant/Site/domain and selected half-open UTC window. The W1 read selects the latest immutable S4-01 FindingRevision of each canonical Finding from one consistent S3/S4 basis, includes every intersecting lifecycle state, and uses strict half-open overlap. Its stable row key is `(tenant_id, site_id, finding_id)`; default order is episode start descending, then Finding UUID ascending. Return exact revision/detail and assessment/evidence lineage, scoped accepted-source and analytic/matcher watermarks, selection token, built time, coverage and typed currentness. Only complete current scoped discovery may state zero Findings; pending, stale, failed and unassessed reads remain visibly Unknown or historical. A changed basis invalidates the bound page cursor. S4-04 must independently prove a watermarked assessed zero-row fixture and persisted positive-row fixture, exact links after reload, invalidation, Site isolation and narrow/desktop UI. Index rows do not depend on money; optional current consequence display consumes LR-01 money eligibility and S4-05 exact-revision source provenance after their reviewed branches enter the build baseline, plus the existing exact-head, quantity, price and currentness gates. Mixed/unknown source and withheld/pending eligibility expose no current amount. S5-02 reuses the Site/window rows for both `mini:3` variants after S4-04 and S5-01 review; S4-03, S5-01's other panels and S3 dispatch do not wait on the index. Full contract and implementation handoff: `.agent/architect-A-finding-index-answers.md`. This decision grants no owner UI copy or walkthrough acceptance and does not implement a global or cross-domain index.

+# Architect answer - Site FindingIndex (2026-10-15)
+
+Decision: `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`. Resolve `A-finding-index` for the W1 Site read only. Authority is the owner-absence work order item 9, the reviewed S4-01 episode contract, `D-2026-10-14-s5-selected-window-overview`, and `D-2026-10-15-s501-finding-choice`. This decision defines a reusable row contract; it does not implement an estate/global index, cold-chain or EV analytics, or approve UI copy.
+
+## Scope and row selection
+
+- Require an authenticated tenant, resolved Site UUID/code, domain and selected half-open UTC window `[from,to)`. The W1 producer is mini-grid dispatch. Resolve the Site within the tenant and use the established 401/403/404 identity rules. Do not silently expand to another Site, another domain or the mock's global Findings area. Echo the requested window and display timezone; changing dates makes a new scoped read, not a client-side filter of an old list.
+- Start with persisted S4-01 Finding and immutable FindingRevision records, never a UI seed, S3 interval alone, Lab private state or a financial consequence. Select the latest permitted revision of each canonical Site Finding from one consistent S3/S4 basis. A row qualifies only when its selected episode intersects the window (`episode_start < to` and `episode_end > from`); a boundary touch does not qualify. Apply S4 merge canonicalization: one row for the surviving ID, with superseded IDs available as relationship/history links. Extension changes the revision on the same Finding; separated recurrence uses a new Finding ID and recurrence link. Closed and unconfirmed episodes remain eligible historical rows in an intersecting window; the default list is not an implicit open-only filter.
+- Row identity is `(tenant_id, site_id, finding_id)` with the stable Finding UUID, canonical ID, exact selected `finding_revision_id`, domain, episode bounds, lifecycle/transition, selected assessment/run/model/cohort and cited accepted-evidence/configuration IDs. Include limitations and exact Site detail URL `/sites/{site_code}/findings/{finding_id}?revision_id={revision_id}`; the API may also return an unpinned canonical URL. The selected revision link must survive reload and later revision creation. A row's source and analytic lineage is the selected immutable revision's lineage, not a recomputation from its title or latest unrelated consequence.
+
+## Ordering and pagination
+
+- Do not infer a priority or confidence rank from the v6.9 illustrative rows. W1 default order is selected `episode_start` descending, then stable Finding UUID ascending. A future reviewed priority rule may add a versioned `priority_tuple`; until then any priority/lens control without a supported producer is hidden or explicitly unavailable. Never order by mutable amount or arrival time. Return a deterministic total count and opaque cursor bound to tenant, Site, domain, window, selected basis/watermark and last sort key. A changed basis invalidates the cursor and requires a fresh first page; it must not splice different revision sets together. Page size is bounded. S5-02 consumes the same ordered Site rows and does not independently rank or select an S5-01 card Finding.
+
+## Watermark and currentness
+
+- Return a read-level selection token/basis ID, accepted-source watermark, S3 assessment selection and analytic watermark, S4 matcher/selection watermark, model/matcher version, `built_at`, coverage/limitations and a typed status. A transactionally consistent scoped read or atomically published projection is acceptable. `knowledge_at` is the stated selection cutoff; do not promise arbitrary commit-ordered historical replay from the current S3 worker clock. Exact revision IDs are the audit anchors.
+- `CURRENT` requires complete scoped discovery and canonical matching through the selected accepted-evidence/configuration/model basis, with no affected dirty, leased or failed S3/S4 work, no watermark mismatch and no moving selection. Relevant late evidence or changed configuration/model invalidates affected windows and must rebuild or make them stale. `PENDING` means no usable completed selection yet; `STALE_PROJECTION` retains prior rows/IDs with the reason while a newer basis is unresolved; `FAILED` reports failed work and any prior selected rows as historical. `UNASSESSED` means no completed assessment/discovery basis. None of these statuses authorizes a current count, current empty claim or an inferred Healthy state. A source-coverage limitation may coexist with a current, complete assessment of the available evidence; state the limitation instead of calling the physical world complete.
+- Keep list freshness separate from each row's assessment claim and from money eligibility. A current list can include an `UNCONFIRMED` Finding or a row with no current amount. A stale list cannot silently replace its selected revision links with newer detail heads.
+
+## Independent fixture proof
+
+- `D-site-finding-index-empty`: persist a tenant/Site/domain/window with an accepted-source and S3/S4 selection watermark, complete assessed discovery, zero canonical intersecting rows and a `CURRENT` zero-row response. Prove a wrong Site/window is excluded and a boundary-touch episode is excluded. Separately exercise unassessed, dirty/stale and failed reads, each visibly Unknown/pending rather than a no-Findings assertion. A filtered empty page after a positive unfiltered read is not the zero-row fixture.
+- `D-site-finding-index-rows`: use committed accepted evidence and S4-01 revisions to persist at least one intersecting canonical Finding, ideally multiple for order and cursor proof. Verify stable ID, latest selected revision, exact detail/history link after reload, recurrence versus extension and canonical merge behavior, cross-Site isolation, date change, boundary intersection and invalidation after a late revision. Empty and positive fixtures are separate W1 closeouts; both must be reviewed. Do not count the mock's F-108 or F-109 as produced Findings without their own reviewed analytics.
+
+## Money and provenance
+
+- A row's existence, order, lifecycle and links are independent of money. The optional consequence cell may use only the exact selected FindingRevision and compatible selected-window consequence. Consume the shared LR-01 `money_eligibility` state and S4-05 exact accepted-ID publication provenance when those reviewed but currently unmerged branches become the build baseline. `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` or `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, mixed/unknown source, stale list or consequence, wrong revision/window, unpriced quantity/price or unresolved source-open state means no current amount. Preserve an old value only behind an explicit historical/audit link. Coherent authored or registered simulation must carry its truthful label and still pass exact-head, currentness and quantity/price gates. Do not aggregate, sum overlapping Findings, infer savings, or turn missing price into zero. If S4-04 starts before those branches merge, leave the consequence cell unavailable and add the reviewed contracts before any current-money presentation or S5-02 integration.
+
+## S4-04 and S5-02 handoff
+
+S4-04 builds the authenticated Site/window index read or equivalent projection, affected-work invalidation, stable cursor and exact revision links, then the Site Findings route/list with selected scope, loading/stale/failed/empty/positive states and keyboard navigation. Reconcile the existing `findings_over` helper: its inclusive end comparison and ascending order do not implement this decision. Its selected revisions must share a consistent matcher/assessment fence; a helper alone is not proof of complete discovery. Compare the supported Site screen at desktop and narrow widths, but do not copy unsupported mock rows, global-list links, Cost/Risks/Opportunities filters, priority/confidence badges or open counts without real producers. The reviewer verifies both fixtures independently, same-basis pagination, tenant isolation, reload and exact drill-down. Owner walkthrough and copy acceptance remain pending until performed.
+
+S5-02 consumes this S4-04 contract on the S5-01 selected Site/window and preserves its own scope, watermark and exact row links. It proves `mini:3#a` honest empty/Unknown and `mini:3#b` positive rows independently. S5-01 may retain a known selected Finding card link but does not close either list variant. S4-03 Evidence/History, `mini:1/2/4` and S3 dispatch do not acquire this index as a prerequisite.

+

+# Architect answer - Site FindingIndex (2026-10-15)
+
+Decision: `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`. Resolve `A-finding-index` for the W1 Site read only. Authority is the owner-absence work order item 9, the reviewed S4-01 episode contract, `D-2026-10-14-s5-selected-window-overview`, and `D-2026-10-15-s501-finding-choice`. This decision defines a reusable row contract; it does not implement an estate/global index, cold-chain or EV analytics, or approve UI copy.
+
+## Scope and row selection
+
+- Require an authenticated tenant, resolved Site UUID/code, domain and selected half-open UTC window `[from,to)`. The W1 producer is mini-grid dispatch. Resolve the Site within the tenant and use the established 401/403/404 identity rules. Do not silently expand to another Site, another domain or the mock's global Findings area. Echo the requested window and display timezone; changing dates makes a new scoped read, not a client-side filter of an old list.
+- Start with persisted S4-01 Finding and immutable FindingRevision records, never a UI seed, S3 interval alone, Lab private state or a financial consequence. Select the latest permitted revision of each canonical Site Finding from one consistent S3/S4 basis. A row qualifies only when its selected episode intersects the window (`episode_start < to` and `episode_end > from`); a boundary touch does not qualify. Apply S4 merge canonicalization: one row for the surviving ID, with superseded IDs available as relationship/history links. Extension changes the revision on the same Finding; separated recurrence uses a new Finding ID and recurrence link. Closed and unconfirmed episodes remain eligible historical rows in an intersecting window; the default list is not an implicit open-only filter.
+- Row identity is `(tenant_id, site_id, finding_id)` with the stable Finding UUID, canonical ID, exact selected `finding_revision_id`, domain, episode bounds, lifecycle/transition, selected assessment/run/model/cohort and cited accepted-evidence/configuration IDs. Include limitations and exact Site detail URL `/sites/{site_code}/findings/{finding_id}?revision_id={revision_id}`; the API may also return an unpinned canonical URL. The selected revision link must survive reload and later revision creation. A row's source and analytic lineage is the selected immutable revision's lineage, not a recomputation from its title or latest unrelated consequence.
+
+## Ordering and pagination
+
+- Do not infer a priority or confidence rank from the v6.9 illustrative rows. W1 default order is selected `episode_start` descending, then stable Finding UUID ascending. A future reviewed priority rule may add a versioned `priority_tuple`; until then any priority/lens control without a supported producer is hidden or explicitly unavailable. Never order by mutable amount or arrival time. Return a deterministic total count and opaque cursor bound to tenant, Site, domain, window, selected basis/watermark and last sort key. A changed basis invalidates the cursor and requires a fresh first page; it must not splice different revision sets together. Page size is bounded. S5-02 consumes the same ordered Site rows and does not independently rank or select an S5-01 card Finding.
+
+## Watermark and currentness
+
+- Return a read-level selection token/basis ID, accepted-source watermark, S3 assessment selection and analytic watermark, S4 matcher/selection watermark, model/matcher version, `built_at`, coverage/limitations and a typed status. A transactionally consistent scoped read or atomically published projection is acceptable. `knowledge_at` is the stated selection cutoff; do not promise arbitrary commit-ordered historical replay from the current S3 worker clock. Exact revision IDs are the audit anchors.
+- `CURRENT` requires complete scoped discovery and canonical matching through the selected accepted-evidence/configuration/model basis, with no affected dirty, leased or failed S3/S4 work, no watermark mismatch and no moving selection. Relevant late evidence or changed configuration/model invalidates affected windows and must rebuild or make them stale. `PENDING` means no usable completed selection yet; `STALE_PROJECTION` retains prior rows/IDs with the reason while a newer basis is unresolved; `FAILED` reports failed work and any prior selected rows as historical. `UNASSESSED` means no completed assessment/discovery basis. None of these statuses authorizes a current count, current empty claim or an inferred Healthy state. A source-coverage limitation may coexist with a current, complete assessment of the available evidence; state the limitation instead of calling the physical world complete.
+- Keep list freshness separate from each row's assessment claim and from money eligibility. A current list can include an `UNCONFIRMED` Finding or a row with no current amount. A stale list cannot silently replace its selected revision links with newer detail heads.
+
+## Independent fixture proof
+
+- `D-site-finding-index-empty`: persist a tenant/Site/domain/window with an accepted-source and S3/S4 selection watermark, complete assessed discovery, zero canonical intersecting rows and a `CURRENT` zero-row response. Prove a wrong Site/window is excluded and a boundary-touch episode is excluded. Separately exercise unassessed, dirty/stale and failed reads, each visibly Unknown/pending rather than a no-Findings assertion. A filtered empty page after a positive unfiltered read is not the zero-row fixture.
+- `D-site-finding-index-rows`: use committed accepted evidence and S4-01 revisions to persist at least one intersecting canonical Finding, ideally multiple for order and cursor proof. Verify stable ID, latest selected revision, exact detail/history link after reload, recurrence versus extension and canonical merge behavior, cross-Site isolation, date change, boundary intersection and invalidation after a late revision. Empty and positive fixtures are separate W1 closeouts; both must be reviewed. Do not count the mock's F-108 or F-109 as produced Findings without their own reviewed analytics.
+
+## Money and provenance
+
+- A row's existence, order, lifecycle and links are independent of money. The optional consequence cell may use only the exact selected FindingRevision and compatible selected-window consequence. Consume the shared LR-01 `money_eligibility` state and S4-05 exact accepted-ID publication provenance when those reviewed but currently unmerged branches become the build baseline. `PENDING_REASSESSMENT` or `WITHHELD_GENERATOR_SERIES`, mixed/unknown source, stale list or consequence, wrong revision/window, unpriced quantity/price or unresolved source-open state means no current amount. Preserve an old value only behind an explicit historical/audit link. Coherent authored or registered simulation must carry its truthful label and still pass exact-head, currentness and quantity/price gates. Do not aggregate, sum overlapping Findings, infer savings, or turn missing price into zero. If S4-04 starts before those branches merge, leave the consequence cell unavailable and add the reviewed contracts before any current-money presentation or S5-02 integration.
+
+## S4-04 and S5-02 handoff
+
+S4-04 builds the authenticated Site/window index read or equivalent projection, affected-work invalidation, stable cursor and exact revision links, then the Site Findings route/list with selected scope, loading/stale/failed/empty/positive states and keyboard navigation. Reconcile the existing `findings_over` helper: its inclusive end comparison and ascending order do not implement this decision. Its selected revisions must share a consistent matcher/assessment fence; a helper alone is not proof of complete discovery. Compare the supported Site screen at desktop and narrow widths, but do not copy unsupported mock rows, global-list links, Cost/Risks/Opportunities filters, priority/confidence badges or open counts without real producers. The reviewer verifies both fixtures independently, same-basis pagination, tenant isolation, reload and exact drill-down. Owner walkthrough and copy acceptance remain pending until performed.
+
+S5-02 consumes this S4-04 contract on the S5-01 selected Site/window and preserves its own scope, watermark and exact row links. It proves `mini:3#a` honest empty/Unknown and `mini:3#b` positive rows independently. S5-01 may retain a known selected Finding card link but does not close either list variant. S4-03 Evidence/History, `mini:1/2/4` and S3 dispatch do not acquire this index as a prerequisite.

+Decision: `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`. Under the owner-absence work order item 9, resolve `A-finding-index` for a tenant/Site/domain and selected half-open UTC window. The W1 read selects the latest immutable S4-01 FindingRevision of each canonical Finding from one consistent S3/S4 basis, includes every intersecting lifecycle state, and uses strict half-open overlap. Its stable row key is `(tenant_id, site_id, finding_id)`; default order is episode start descending, then Finding UUID ascending. Return exact revision/detail and assessment/evidence lineage, scoped accepted-source and analytic/matcher watermarks, selection token, built time, coverage and typed currentness. Mixed/unknown source and withheld/pending eligibility expose no current amount. S5-02 reuses the Site/window rows for both `mini:3` variants after S4-04 and S5-01 review; S4-03, S5-01's other panels and S3 dispatch do not wait on the index. Full contract and implementation handoff: `.agent/architect-A-finding-index-answers.md`. This decision grants no owner UI copy or walkthrough acceptance and does not implement a global or cross-domain index.
